It's commonly used for reading game data files, including Civilization 6 map files which are stored as SQLite databases.\n\n## Installation\nNo installation required - `sqlite3` is part of Python's standard library.\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Connecting to a Database\n```python\nimport sqlite3\n\n# Connect to database\nconn = sqlite3.connect('path/to/database.db')\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Always close when done\nconn.close()\n```\n\n### Querying Data\n```python\n# Execute a query\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM TableName\")\n\n# Fetch results\nrows = cursor.fetchall()  # All rows\nrow = cursor.fetchone()   # Single row\n\n# Query with parameters (safe from SQL injection)\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM Table WHERE id = ?\", (value,))\n```\n\n### Common Patterns for Civ6 Maps\n\n#### Get Map Dimensions\n```python\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map\")\nwidth, height = cursor.fetchone()\n```\n\n#### Get All Plots\n```python\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\nplots = cursor.fetchall()\n```\n\n#### Get Plot Features (mountains, forests, etc.)\n```python\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures\")\nfeatures = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n```\n\n#### Get Plot Resources\n```python\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources\")\nresources = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n```\n\n#### Convert Plot ID to Coordinates\n```python\ndef plot_id_to_coords(plot_id, width):\n    \"\"\"Convert linear plot ID to (x, y) coordinates.\"\"\"\n    x = plot_id % width\n    y = plot_id // width\n    return (x, y)\n\ndef coords_to_plot_id(x, y, width):\n    \"\"\"Convert (x, y) coordinates to linear plot ID.\"\"\"\n    return y * width + x\n```\n\n## Working with Row Data\n\n### Using Row Factory for Dict-like Access\n```python\nconn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\ncursor = conn.cursor()\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM Plots\")\nfor row in cursor:\n    print(row['TerrainType'])  # Access by column name\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Use context managers** to ensure proper cleanup:\n```python\nwith sqlite3.connect('database.db') as conn:\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n    # Do work here\n```\n\n2. **Use parameterized queries** to prevent SQL injection:\n```python\n# Good\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM Table WHERE id = ?\", (user_input,))\n\n# Bad\ncursor.execute(f\"SELECT * FROM Table WHERE id = {user_input}\")\n```\n\n3. **Check if values exist** before accessing:\n```python\nresult = cursor.fetchone()\nif result:\n    value = result[0]\n```\n\n## Common Table Structures in Civ6 Maps\n\n- **Map**: Width, Height, WrapX, WrapY\n- **Plots**: ID, TerrainType, ContinentType, IsImpassable\n- **PlotFeatures**: ID, FeatureType (mountains, forests, etc.)\n- **PlotResources**: ID, ResourceType, ResourceCount\n- **PlotRivers**: ID, IsNEOfRiver, IsWOfRiver, IsNWOfRiver\n- **PlotCliffs**: ID, IsNEOfCliff, IsWOfCliff, IsNWOfCliff\n\n## Example: Reading a Civ6 Map\n```python\nimport sqlite3\n\ndef load_civ6_map(map_path):\n    \"\"\"Load a Civ6 map file and extract key information.\"\"\"\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(map_path)\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n    # Get map size\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map\")\n    width, height = cursor.fetchone()\n\n    # Get terrain data\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n    terrain = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n    # Get features (mountains, etc.)\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures\")\n    features = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    return {\n        'width': width,\n        'height': height,\n        'terrain': terrain,\n        'features': features\n    }\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":110,"cache_read_input_tokens":24379,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":110,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e51a7829-9fba-4b5d-9fb6-3607c1b07435","uuid":"20a76f91-7759-4ff4-ae55-645e74ac8433"}

Access by column name\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Use context managers** to ensure proper cleanup:\n```python\nwith sqlite3.connect('database.db') as conn:\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n    # Do work here\n```\n\n2. **Use parameterized queries** to prevent SQL injection:\n```python\n# Good\ncursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM Table WHERE id = ?\", (user_input,))\n\n# Bad\ncursor.execute(f\"SELECT * FROM Table WHERE id = {user_input}\")\n```\n\n3. **Check if values exist** before accessing:\n```python\nresult = cursor.fetchone()\nif result:\n    value = result[0]\n```\n\n## Common Table Structures in Civ6 Maps\n\n- **Map**: Width, Height, WrapX, WrapY\n- **Plots**: ID, TerrainType, ContinentType, IsImpassable\n- **PlotFeatures**: ID, FeatureType (mountains, forests, etc.)\n- **PlotResources**: ID, ResourceType, ResourceCount\n- **PlotRivers**: ID, IsNEOfRiver, IsWOfRiver, IsNWOfRiver\n- **PlotCliffs**: ID, IsNEOfCliff, IsWOfCliff, IsNWOfCliff\n\n## Example: Reading a Civ6 Map\n```python\nimport sqlite3\n\ndef load_civ6_map(map_path):\n    \"\"\"Load a Civ6 map file and extract key information.\"\"\"\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(map_path)\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n    # Understanding these rules is critical for optimal city planning.\n\n## Core Concepts\n\n### Hexagonal Grid\nCiv6 uses a hexagonal grid. Each tile has 6 neighbors. Coordinates can be represented as (x, y) with special neighbor calculation for hex grids.\n\n### Adjacency Calculation\nEach district type gains bonuses from specific neighboring tiles. Bonuses are cumulative.\n\n## District Types and Adjacency Rules\n\n### CAMPUS (Science)\n- **+2** per adjacent Mountain\n- **+1** per adjacent Rainforest\n- **+1** per adjacent Reef (coastal)\n- **+0.5** per adjacent Great Barrier Reef (rounds down every 2)\n- **-1** per adjacent District (except City Center and Aqueduct)\n\n### HOLY_SITE (Faith)\n- **+2** per adjacent Mountain\n- **+1** per adjacent Woods (Forest)\n- **+1** per adjacent Natural Wonder\n- **-1** per adjacent District (except City Center)\n\n### THEATER_SQUARE (Culture)\n- **+1** per adjacent Wonder (both natural and built)\n- **+0.5** per adjacent District (except City Center) - rounds down every 2\n- Special adjacency from Pamukkale and other natural wonders\n\n### COMMERCIAL_HUB (Gold)\n- **+2** per adjacent River tile\n- **+1** per adjacent Harbor district\n- **+0.5** per adjacent District (except City Center) - rounds down every 2\n- Note: River adjacency means the tile itself is next to a river edge\n\n### HARBOR (Gold)\n- **+2** per adjacent City Center\n- **+1** per adjacent Coastal Resource (fish, crabs, etc.)\n- **+0.5** per adjacent District - rounds down every 2\n- Must be placed on a coastal tile\n\n### INDUSTRIAL_ZONE (Production)\n- **+1** per adjacent Mine\n- **+1** per adjacent Quarry\n- **+1** per adjacent Strategic Resource\n- **+0.5** per adjacent District (except City Center and Aqueduct) - rounds down every 2\n\n### ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX (Amenities)\n- **+2** per adjacent Wonder\n- **+1** per adjacent Entertainment Complex (from other cities)\n- No standard adjacency bonuses from terrain\n\n### ENCAMPMENT (Military)\n- No adjacency bonuses from terrain\n- Gets bonuses from being adjacent to certain terrain for defensive purposes (not counted as adjacency bonus)\n\n### AQUEDUCT (Housing/Freshwater)\n- Not typically counted for adjacency optimization\n- Must be adjacent to City Center and a freshwater source\n\n## Neighbor Calculation in Hexagonal Grids\n\n### Offset Coordinates (Odd-Q Layout)\nCiv6 typically uses offset coordinates where odd columns are shifted down.\n\n```python\ndef get_hex_neighbors(x, y, width, height):\n    \"\"\"Get all valid neighboring tiles in a hex grid (odd-q layout).\"\"\"\n    neighbors = []\n\n    # Odd-q vertical layout\n    if x % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n        offsets = [\n            (1, 0),   # E\n            (1, -1),  # NE\n            (0, -1),  # NW\n            (-1, -1), # W\n            (-1, 0),  # SW\n            (0, 1)    # SE\n        ]\n    else:  # Odd column\n        offsets = [\n            (1, 1),   # E\n            (1, 0),   # NE\n            (0, -1),  # NW\n            (-1, 0),  # W\n            (-1, 1),  # SW\n            (0, 1)    # SE\n        ]\n\n    for dx, dy in offsets:\n        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n        if 0 <= nx < width and 0 <= ny < height:\n            neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n\n    return neighbors\n```\n\n## Calculating Total Adjacency\n\n```python\ndef map_data['height'])\n\n    for nx, ny in neighbors:\n        tile = map_data['tiles'][ny][nx]\n\n        # Mountains: +2\n        if tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_MOUNTAINS':\n            total += 2\n\n        # Rainforest: +1\n        elif tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n            total += 1\n\n        # Reef: +1\n        elif tile.get('feature') == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n            total += 1\n\n        # Other districts: -1 (except City Center, Aqueduct)\n        elif tile.get('district') and tile['district'] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n            total -= 1\n\n    return total\n```\n\n## Important Notes\n\n### Rounding Rules\n- **+0.5** bonuses round down for every 2 instances\n  - 1 district = 0 bonus\n  - 2 districts = 1 bonus\n  - 3 districts = 1 bonus\n  - 4 districts = 2 bonus\n\n### District Restrictions\n- Districts cannot be placed on certain terrain (mountains, natural wonders)\n- Most districts require specific terrain (Harbor requires coast)\n- Districts cannot overlap\n- Each tile can only have one district\n\n### City Center Rules\n- City Center provides adjacency bonuses to some districts\n- City Center does NOT count as a district for negative adjacency\n- All districts must be within 3 tiles of City Center (based on population)\n\n### Population and District Limits\n- Population 1-3: 1 district\n- Population 4-6: 2 districts\n- Population 7-9: 3 districts\n- Population 10-12: 4 districts\n- And so on...\n\nFormula: `max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1`\n\n## River Detection\nRivers in Civ6 exist on tile edges, not on {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Tj8cS71cxgPdNWg6gVqgPF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01FNCQGGxonEah6Pbwv47fim","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/combinatorial-optimization.md","content":"# Combinatorial Optimization for Placement Problems\n\n## Overview\nPlacement optimization problems involve selecting positions and configurations to maximize (or minimize) an objective function. Common in game AI, facility location, and resource allocation.\n\n## Problem Structure\n\n### Key Components\n1. **Decision Variables**: What to place and where\n2. **Objective Function**: What to maximize/minimize (e.g., total adjacency bonus)\n3. **Constraints**: Rules that must be satisfied (e.g., no overlapping, within range)\n\n### Example: District Placement\n- Variables: Location of city center, which districts to build, where to place each\n- Objective: Maximize total adjacency bonuses\n- Constraints: No overlap, within city range, terrain requirements, district limits\n\n## Optimization Approaches\n\n### 1. Greedy Algorithm\n**Best for**: Quick solutions, simple problems\n**Approach**: Make locally optimal choice at each step\n\n```python\ndef greedy_placement(map_data, num_districts):\n    \"\"\"Place districts one at a time, choosing best position for each.\"\"\"\n    placements = {}\n\n    for i in range(num_districts):\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n        best_position = None\n        best_district = None\n\n        # Try each district type\n        for district_type in available_districts:\n            # Try each valid position\n            for x, y in get_valid_positions(map_data, placements):\n                score = calculate_adjacency(district_type, x, y, map_data, placements)\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_position = (x, y)\n                    best_district = district_type\n\n        placements[best_position] = best_district\n\n    return placements\n```\n\n**Pros**: Fast, simple to implement\n**Cons**: May miss global optimum, order-dependent\n\n### 2. Exhaustive Search\n**Best for**: Small search spaces, guaranteed optimal\n**Approach**: Try all possible combinations\n\n```python\nfrom itertools import combinations, permutations\n\ndef exhaustive_search(positions, districts, map_data):\n    \"\"\"Try all possible position-district assignments.\"\"\"\n    best_score = -float('inf')\n    best_solution = None\n\n    # Choose positions for districts\n    for position_combo in combinations(positions, len(districts)):\n        # Try different district assignments\n        for district_perm in permutations(districts):\n            placements = dict(zip(position_combo, district_perm))\n            score = calculate_total_adjacency(placements, map_data)\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_solution = placements\n\n    return best_solution, best_score\n```\n\n**Pros**: Guaranteed optimal\n**Cons**: Exponential time complexity, infeasible for large problems\n\n### 3. Hill Climbing / Local Search\n**Best for**: Medium-sized problems, when greedy isn't enough\n**Approach**: Start with a solution, iteratively improve\n\n```python\nimport random\n\ndef hill_climbing(initial_solution, map_data, max_iterations=1000):\n    \"\"\"Improve solution by making small changes.\"\"\"\n    current = initial_solution.copy()\n    current_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current, map_data)\n\n    for _ in range(max_iterations):\n        # Generate neighbor solution (swap two districts, move one, etc.)\n        neighbor = generate_neighbor(current, map_data)\n        neighbor_score = calculate_total_adjacency(neighbor, map_data)\n\n        # Accept if better\n        if neighbor_score > current_score:\n            current = neighbor\n            current_score = neighbor_score\n\n    return current, current_score\n\ndef generate_neighbor(solution, map_data):\n    \"\"\"Create a slightly modified version of solution.\"\"\"\n    neighbor = solution.copy()\n\n    # Random modification: move a random district to a new position\n    district = random.choice(list(neighbor.keys()))\n    new_pos = random.choice(get_valid_positions(map_data, neighbor))\n\n    del neighbor[district]\n    neighbor[new_pos] = district\n\n    return neighbor\n```\n\n**Pros**: Better than greedy, faster than exhaustive\n**Cons**: Can get stuck in local optima\n\n### 4. Beam Search\n**Best for**: Balance between quality and speed\n**Approach**: Keep top-k solutions at each step\n\n```python\ndef beam_search(map_data, districts, beam_width=10):\n    \"\"\"Explore multiple promising paths simultaneously.\"\"\"\n    # Start with multiple city center positions\n    beam = [(calculate_score({}, map_data), {}) for _ in range(beam_width)]\n\n    for district in districts:\n        candidates = []\n\n        for score, current_placement in beam:\n            # For each solution in beam, try adding district at each position\n            for pos in get_valid_positions(map_data, current_placement):\n                new_placement = current_placement.copy()\n                new_placement[pos] = district\n                new_score = calculate_total_adjacency(new_placement, map_data)\n                candidates.append((new_score, new_placement))\n\n        # Keep only top beam_width solutions\n        candidates.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        beam = candidates[:beam_width]\n\n    return beam[0]  # Return best solution\n```\n\n**Pros**: Better exploration than greedy, manageable complexity\n**Cons**: May still miss optimal, tuning beam width is important\n\n## Optimization Strategies\n\n### Pruning Invalid Solutions Early\n```python\ndef is_valid_placement(district, position, map_data, current_placements):\n    \"\"\"Check if placement satisfies all constraints.\"\"\"\n    x, y = position\n\n    # Check terrain requirements\n    if district == 'HARBOR' and not is_coastal(x, y, map_data):\n        return False\n\n    # Check if position is already occupied\n    if position in current_placements:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if within city range\n    if not within_city_range(position, current_placements.get('CITY_CENTER')):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Prioritizing High-Value Decisions\nPlace districts with the most positional variability first:\n1. City Center (affects all other placements)\n2. Harbor (limited to coastal tiles)\n3. Campus (benefits from mountains)\n4. Other districts\n\n### Caching Calculations\n```python\nadjacency_cache = {}\n\ndef calculate_adjacency_cached(district, pos, map_data, placements):\n    \"\"\"Cache adjacency calculations to avoid recomputation.\"\"\"\n    cache_key = (district, pos, tuple(sorted(placements.items())))\n\n    if cache_key not in adjacency_cache:\n        adjacency_cache[cache_key] = calculate_adjacency(district, pos, map_data, placements)\n\n    return adjacency_cache[cache_key]\n```\n\n## Choosing the Right Approach\n\n| Approach | Time Complexity | Solution Quality | Best For |\n|----------|----------------|------------------|----------|\n| Greedy | O(n²) | Fair | Quick solutions, simple problems |\n| Exhaustive | O(n!) | Optimal | Small problems (< 10 items) |\n| Hill Climbing | O(n * iterations) | Good | Medium problems |\n| Beam Search | O(n² * beam_width) | Good-Excellent | Balancing quality and speed |\n\n## Practical Example: Civ6 District Optimization\n\n```python\ndef optimize_district_placement(map_data, num_cities, population):\n    \"\"\"Main optimization function for Civ6 districts.\"\"\"\n    max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n    # Step 1: Find best city center position\n    best_city_center = find_optimal_city_center(map_data, max_districts)\n\n    # Step 2: Determine which districts to build (greedy selection)\n    districts_to_build = select_districts(map_data, best_city_center, max_districts)\n\n    # Step 3: Use beam search to find optimal placements\n    solution = beam_search_districts(\n        map_data,\n        best_city_center,\n        districts_to_build,\n        beam_width=20\n    )\n\n    return solution\n```\n\n## Key Takeaways\n1. Start simple (greedy), optimize if needed\n2. Understand your constraints and encode them properly\n3. Consider problem size when choosing approach\n4. Cache expensive calculations\n5. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01FNCQGGxonEah6Pbwv47fim","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/environment/skills/combinatorial-optimization.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e51a7829-9fba-4b5d-9fb6-3607c1b07435","uuid":"949b07de-abcf-4c8a-8e59-e0c59ffa9752","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/environment/skills/combinatorial-optimization.md","content":"# Combinatorial Optimization for Placement Problems\n\n## Overview\nPlacement optimization problems involve selecting positions and configurations to maximize (or minimize) an objective function. Common in game AI, facility location, and resource allocation.\n\n## Problem Structure\n\n### Key Components\n1. **Decision Variables**: What to place and where\n2. **Objective Function**: What to maximize/minimize (e.g., total adjacency bonus)\n3. **Constraints**: Rules that must be satisfied (e.g., no overlapping, within range)\n\n### Example: District Placement\n- Variables: Location of city center, which districts to build, where to place each\n- Objective: Maximize total adjacency bonuses\n- Constraints: No overlap, within city range, terrain requirements, district limits\n\n## Optimization Approaches\n\n### 1. Greedy Algorithm\n**Best for**: Quick solutions, simple problems\n**Approach**: Make locally optimal choice at each step\n\n```python\ndef greedy_placement(map_data, num_districts):\n    \"\"\"Place districts one at a time, choosing best position for each.\"\"\"\n    placements = {}\n\n    for i in range(num_districts):\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n        best_position = None\n        best_district = None\n\n        # Try each district type\n        for district_type in available_districts:\n            # Try each valid position\n            for x, y in get_valid_positions(map_data, placements):\n                score = calculate_adjacency(district_type, x, y, map_data, placements)\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_position = (x, y)\n                    best_district = district_type\n\n        placements[best_position] = best_district\n\n    return placements\n```\n\n**Pros**: Fast, simple to implement\n**Cons**: May miss global optimum, order-dependent\n\n### 2. Exhaustive Search\n**Best for**: Small search spaces, guaranteed optimal\n**Approach**: Try all possible combinations\n\n```python\nfrom itertools import combinations, permutations\n\ndef exhaustive_search(positions, districts, map_data):\n    \"\"\"Try all possible position-district assignments.\"\"\"\n    best_score = -float('inf')\n    best_solution = None\n\n    # Choose positions for districts\n    for position_combo in combinations(positions, len(districts)):\n        # Try different district assignments\n        for district_perm in permutations(districts):\n            placements = dict(zip(position_combo, district_perm))\n            score = calculate_total_adjacency(placements, map_data)\n\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_solution = placements\n\n    return best_solution, best_score\n```\n\n**Pros**: Guaranteed optimal\n**Cons**: Exponential time complexity, infeasible for large problems\n\n### 3. Hill Climbing / Local Search\n**Best for**: Medium-sized problems, when greedy isn't enough\n**Approach**: Start with a solution, iteratively improve\n\n```python\nimport random\n\ndef hill_climbing(initial_solution, map_data, max_iterations=1000):\n    \"\"\"Improve solution by making small changes.\"\"\"\n    current = initial_solution.copy()\n    current_score = calculate_total_adjacency(current, map_data)\n\n    for _ in range(max_iterations):\n        # Generate neighbor solution (swap two districts, move one, etc.)\n        neighbor = generate_neighbor(current, map_data)\n        neighbor_score = calculate_total_adjacency(neighbor, map_data)\n\n        # Accept if better\n        if neighbor_score > current_score:\n            current = neighbor\n            current_score = neighbor_score\n\n    return current, current_score\n\ndef generate_neighbor(solution, map_data):\n    \"\"\"Create a slightly modified version of solution.\"\"\"\n    neighbor = solution.copy()\n\n    # Random modification: move a random district to a new position\n    district = random.choice(list(neighbor.keys()))\n    new_pos = random.choice(get_valid_positions(map_data, neighbor))\n\n    del neighbor[district]\n    neighbor[new_pos] = district\n\n    return neighbor\n```\n\n**Pros**: Better than greedy, faster than exhaustive\n**Cons**: Can get stuck in local optima\n\n### 4. Beam Search\n**Best for**: Balance between quality and speed\n**Approach**: Keep top-k solutions at each step\n\n```python\ndef beam_search(map_data, districts, beam_width=10):\n    \"\"\"Explore multiple promising paths simultaneously.\"\"\"\n    # Start with multiple city center positions\n    beam = [(calculate_score({}, map_data), {}) for _ in range(beam_width)]\n\n    for district in districts:\n        candidates = []\n\n        for score, current_placement in beam:\n            # For each solution in beam, try adding district at each position\n            for pos in get_valid_positions(map_data, current_placement):\n                new_placement = current_placement.copy()\n                new_placement[pos] = district\n                new_score = calculate_total_adjacency(new_placement, map_data)\n                candidates.append((new_score, new_placement))\n\n        # Keep only top beam_width solutions\n        candidates.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        beam = candidates[:beam_width]\n\n    return beam[0]  # Return best solution\n```\n\n**Pros**: Better exploration than greedy, manageable complexity\n**Cons**: May still miss optimal, tuning beam width is important\n\n## Optimization Strategies\n\n### Pruning Invalid Solutions Early\n```python\ndef is_valid_placement(district, position, map_data, current_placements):\n    \"\"\"Check if placement satisfies all constraints.\"\"\"\n    x, y = position\n\n    # Check terrain requirements\n    if district == 'HARBOR' and not is_coastal(x, y, map_data):\n        return False\n\n    # Check if position is already occupied\n    if position in current_placements:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if within city range\n    if not within_city_range(position, current_placements.get('CITY_CENTER')):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Prioritizing High-Value Decisions\nPlace districts with the most positional variability first:\n1. City Center (affects all other placements)\n2. Harbor (limited to coastal tiles)\n3. Campus (benefits from mountains)\n4. Other districts\n\n### Caching Calculations\n```python\nadjacency_cache = {}\n\ndef calculate_adjacency_cached(district, pos, map_data, placements):\n    \"\"\"Cache adjacency calculations to avoid recomputation.\"\"\"\n    cache_key = (district, pos, tuple(sorted(placements.items())))\n\n    if cache_key not in adjacency_cache:\n        adjacency_cache[cache_key] = calculate_adjacency(district, pos, map_data, placements)\n\n    return adjacency_cache[cache_key]\n```\n\n## Choosing the Right Approach\n\n| Approach | Time Complexity | Solution Quality | Best For |\n|----------|----------------|------------------|----------|\n| Greedy | O(n²) | Fair | Quick solutions, simple problems |\n| Exhaustive | O(n!) | Optimal | Small problems (< 10 items) |\n| Hill Climbing | O(n * iterations) | Good | Medium problems |\n| Beam Search | O(n² * beam_width) | Good-Excellent | Balancing quality and speed |\n\n## Practical Example: Civ6 District Optimization\n\n```python\ndef optimize_district_placement(map_data, num_cities, population):\n    \"\"\"Main optimization function for Civ6 districts.\"\"\"\n    max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n    # Step 1: Find best city center position\n    best_city_center = find_optimal_city_center(map_data, max_districts)\n\n    # Step 2: Determine which districts to build (greedy selection)\n    districts_to_build = select_districts(map_data, best_city_center, max_districts)\n\n    # Step 3: Use beam search to find optimal placements\n    solution = beam_search_districts(\n        map_data,\n        best_city_center,\n        districts_to_build,\n        beam_width=20\n    )\n\n    return solution\n```\n\n## Key Takeaways\n1. Start simple (greedy), optimize if needed\n2. Understand your constraints and encode them properly\n3. Consider problem size when choosing approach\n4. Cache expensive calculations\n5. Odd-q vertical layout - odd columns shifted down\n        if x % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 0),   # E\n                (1, -1),  # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, -1), # W\n                (-1, 0),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 1),   # E\n                (1, 0),   # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                    total += 1\n                # Reef: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Woods/Forest: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_FOREST':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Districts (not City Center): +0.5 each (rounds down every 2)\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total = district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # River: +2 per adjacent river tile\n                if self.map.has_river(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Harbor district: +1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'HARBOR':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'HARBOR':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Convert offset to cube coordinates\n        def offset_to_cube(x, y):\n            q = x\n            r = y - (x - (x & 1)) // 2\n            s = -q - r\n            return (q, r, s)\n\n        q1, r1, s1 = offset_to_cube(x1, y1)\n        q2, r2, s2 = offset_to_cube(x2, y2)\n\n        return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(r1 - r2) + abs(s1 - s2)) // 2\n\n    def optimize_single_city(self, population: int) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Optimize district placement for a single city.\"\"\"\n        max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n        # Get all valid positions within city range\n        valid_positions = []\n        cx, cy = city_center\n        for y in range(max(0, cy - 3), min(self.map.height, cy + 4)):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if (x, y) == city_center:\n                    continue\n                for district in self.DISTRICTS:\n                    if self.is_valid_placement(district, x, y, city_center, placements):\n                        valid_positions.append((x, y))\n                        break  # Place the best district found\n            if best_district and best_position:\n                placements[best_position] = best_district\n                adjacency_bonuses[best_district] = best_score\n\n        # Remove city center from placements for output\n        del placements[city_center]\n\n        total = sum(adjacency_bonuses.values())\n\n        return {\n            'placements': placements,\n            'adjacency_bonuses': adjacency_bonuses,\n            'total_adjacency': total\n        }, total\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path: str, output_path: str):\n    \"\"\"Solve a Civ6 district placement scenario.\"\"\"\n    # NW\n                (-1, -1), # W\n                (-1, 0),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 1),   # E\n                (1, 0),   # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, 0),  # W\n                (-1, 1),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            # Handle wrap-around for X if needed\n            if nx < 0:\n                nx += self.width\n            elif nx >= self.width:\n                nx -= self.width\n\n            # Check Y bounds (no wrap)\n            if 0 <= ny < self.height:\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n\n        return neighbors\n\n    def is_mountain(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is a mountain.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n    def is_coastal(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is coastal (not ocean, but adjacent to ocean/coast).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain == 'TERRAIN_COAST'\n\n    def is_land(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is buildable land (not water, not mountain).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain not in ['TERRAIN_OCEAN', 'TERRAIN_COAST'] and 'MOUNTAIN' not in terrain\n\n    def has_river(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is adjacent to a river.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return plot_id in self.rivers\n\n    def get_feature(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get feature at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.features.get(plot_id)\n\n    def get_resource(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get resource at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.resources.get(plot_id)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimizes district placement for maximum adjacency bonuses.\"\"\"\n\n    # District types that can be built\n    DISTRICTS = [\n        'CAMPUS',\n        'HOLY_SITE',\n        'THEATER_SQUARE',\n        'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n        'HARBOR',\n        'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE',\n        'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX'\n    ]\n\n    def __init__(self, civ_map: Civ6Map):\n        self.map = civ_map\n\n    def calculate_adjacency(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str]) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at position (x, y).\"\"\"\n        neighbors = self.map.get_hex_neighbors(x, y)\n        total = 0\n\n        if district == 'CAMPUS':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                    total += 1\n                # Reef: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Woods/Forest: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_FOREST':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Districts (not City Center): +0.5 each (rounds down every 2)\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total = district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # River: +2 per adjacent river tile\n                if self.map.has_river(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Harbor district: +1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'HARBOR':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'HARBOR':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # City Center: +2\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total += 2\n                # Coastal resources: +1\n                elif self.map.is_coastal(nx, ny) and self.map.get_resource(nx, ny):\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts: +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Strategic resources: +1\n                resource = self.map.get_resource(nx, ny)\n                if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            # No standard adjacency bonuses from terrain\n            pass\n\n        return total\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str],\n                          max_range: int = 3) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a district can be placed at (x, y).\"\"\"\n        # Can't place on occupied tile\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        # Harbor must be on coastal tile\n        if district == 'HARBOR':\n            if not self.map.is_coastal(x, y):\n                return False\n        else:\n            # Other districts must be on land\n            if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                return False\n\n        # Must be within range of city center\n        if city_center:\n            cx, cy = city_center\n            # Calculate hex distance (simplified - use Manhattan as approximation)\n            distance = self.hex_distance(x, y, cx, cy)\n            if distance > max_range:\n                return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def hex_distance(self, x1: int, y1: int, x2: int, y2: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate distance between two hex tiles.\"\"\"\n        # Convert offset to cube coordinates\n        def offset_to_cube(x, y):\n            q = x\n            r = y - (x - (x & 1)) // 2\n            s = -q - r\n            return (q, r, s)\n\n        q1, r1, s1 = offset_to_cube(x1, y1)\n        q2, r2, s2 = offset_to_cube(x2, y2)\n\n        return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(r1 - r2) + abs(s1 - s2)) // 2\n\n    def optimize_single_city(self, population: int) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Optimize district placement for a single city.\"\"\"\n        max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n        # Find best city center position\n        best_city_center = None\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        # Try different city center positions on land tiles\n        for y in range(self.map.height):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                city_center = (x, y)\n                solution, score = self.optimize_districts_for_city(\n                    city_center, max_districts\n                )\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_city_center = city_center\n                    best_solution = solution\n\n        return {\n            'city_center': list(best_city_center),\n            'placements': {k: list(v) for k, v in best_solution['placements'].items()},\n            'adjacency_bonuses': best_solution['adjacency_bonuses'],\n            'total_adjacency': best_solution['total_adjacency']\n        }\n\n    def optimize_districts_for_city(self, city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                                   max_districts: int) -> Tuple[Dict, int]:\n        \"\"\"Optimize which districts to build and where for a given city center.\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n\n        # Get all valid positions within city range\n        valid_positions = []\n        cx, cy = city_center\n        for y in range(max(0, cy - 3), min(self.map.height, cy + 4)):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if (x, y) == city_center:\n                    continue\n                for district in self.DISTRICTS:\n                    if self.is_valid_placement(district, x, y, city_center, placements):\n                        valid_positions.append((x, y))\n                        break  # Only need to check if position is valid for any district\n\n        # Remove duplicates\n        valid_positions = list(set(valid_positions))\n\n        # Use greedy approach with lookahead for optimization\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        # Try different district combinations\n        for num_districts in range(min(max_districts, len(valid_positions)), 0, -1):\n            solution, score = self.greedy_optimization(\n                city_center, valid_positions, num_districts\n            )\n            if score > best_score:\n                best_score = score\n                best_solution = solution\n\n        return best_solution, best_score\n\n    def greedy_optimization(self, city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                          valid_positions: List[Tuple[int, int]],\n                          num_districts: int) -> Tuple[Dict, int]:\n        \"\"\"Use greedy algorithm to place districts.\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n        adjacency_bonuses = {}\n\n        for _ in range(num_districts):\n            best_score = -float('inf')\n            best_district = None\n            best_position = None\n\n            # Try each district type at each valid position\n            for district in self.DISTRICTS:\n                for pos in valid_positions:\n                    if not self.is_valid_placement(district, pos[0], pos[1],\n                                                  city_center, placements):\n                        continue\n\n                    score = self.calculate_adjacency(district, pos[0], pos[1], placements)\n\n                    if score > best_score:\n                        best_score = score\n                        best_district = district\n                        best_position = pos\n\n            # Place the best district found\n            if best_district and best_position:\n                placements[best_position] = best_district\n                adjacency_bonuses[best_district] = best_score\n\n        # Remove city center from placements for output\n        del placements[city_center]\n\n        total = sum(adjacency_bonuses.values())\n\n        return {\n            'placements': placements,\n            'adjacency_bonuses': adjacency_bonuses,\n            'total_adjacency': total\n        }, total\n\n\ndef solve_scenario(scenario_path: str, output_path: str):\n    \"\"\"Solve a Civ6 district placement scenario.\"\"\"\n    # Load scenario\n    with open(scenario_path, 'r') as f:\n        scenario = json.load(f)\n\n    # Load map\n    map_path = f\"/data/maps/{scenario['map_file'].split('/')[-1]}\"\n    civ_map = Civ6Map(map_path)\n\n    # Create optimizer\n    optimizer = DistrictOptimizer(civ_map)\n\n    # Solve for single city\n    if scenario['num_cities'] == 1:\n        solution = optimizer.optimize_single_city(scenario['population'])\n    else:\n        raise NotImplementedError(\"Multi-city optimization not yet implemented\")\n\n    # Write solution\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(solution, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Solution written to {output_path}\")\n    print(f\"Total adjacency bonus: {solution['total_adjacency']}\")\n    print(f\"City center: {solution['city_center']}\")\n    print(f\"Districts placed: {len(solution['placements'])}\")\n    for district, pos in solution['placements'].items():\n        bonus = solution['adjacency_bonuses'].get(district, 0)\n        print(f\"  {district} at {pos}: +{bonus}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    solve_scenario('/data/scenario_3/scenario.json', '/output/scenario_3.json')\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Get map dimensions\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map\")\n        self.width, self.height = cursor.fetchone()\n\n        # Load terrain\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        self.terrain = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n        # Load features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures\")\n        self.features = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n        # Load rivers\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, IsNEOfRiver, IsWOfRiver, IsNWOfRiver FROM PlotRivers\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, ne, w, nw = row\n            if ne or w or nw:\n                self.rivers[plot_id] = {'ne': ne, 'w': w, 'nw': nw}\n\n        # Odd-q vertical layout - odd columns shifted down\n        if x % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 0),   # E\n                (1, -1),  # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, -1), # W\n                (-1, 0),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 1),   # E\n                (1, 0),   # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, 0),  # W\n                (-1, 1),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            # Handle wrap-around for X if needed\n            if nx < 0:\n                nx += self.width\n            elif nx >= self.width:\n                nx -= self.width\n\n            # Check Y bounds (no wrap)\n            if 0 <= ny < self.height:\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n\n        return neighbors\n\n    def is_mountain(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is a mountain.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n    def is_coastal(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is coastal (not ocean, but adjacent to ocean/coast).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain == 'TERRAIN_COAST'\n\n    def is_land(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is buildable land (not water, not mountain).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain not in ['TERRAIN_OCEAN', 'TERRAIN_COAST'] and 'MOUNTAIN' not in terrain\n\n    def has_river(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is adjacent to a river.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return plot_id in self.rivers\n\n    def get_feature(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get feature at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.features.get(plot_id)\n\n    def get_resource(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get resource at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.resources.get(plot_id)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimizes district placement for maximum adjacency bonuses.\"\"\"\n\n    # District types that can be built\n    DISTRICTS = [\n        'CAMPUS',\n        'HOLY_SITE',\n        'THEATER_SQUARE',\n        'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n        'HARBOR',\n        'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE',\n        'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX'\n    ]\n\n    def __init__(self, civ_map: Civ6Map):\n        self.map = civ_map\n\n    def calculate_adjacency(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str]) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at position (x, y).\"\"\"\n        neighbors = self.map.get_hex_neighbors(x, y)\n        total = 0\n\n        if district == 'CAMPUS':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                    total += 1\n                # Reef: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Woods/Forest: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_FOREST':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Districts (not City Center): +0.5 each (rounds down every 2)\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total = district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # River: +2 per adjacent river tile\n                if self.map.has_river(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Harbor district: +1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'HARBOR':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'HARBOR':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # City Center: +2\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total += 2\n                # Coastal resources: +1\n                elif self.map.is_coastal(nx, ny) and self.map.get_resource(nx, ny):\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts: +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Strategic resources: +1\n                resource = self.map.get_resource(nx, ny)\n                if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            # No standard adjacency bonuses from terrain\n            pass\n\n        return total\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str],\n                          max_range: int = 3) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a district can be placed at (x, y).\"\"\"\n        # Can't place on occupied tile\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        # Harbor must be on coastal tile\n        if district == 'HARBOR':\n            if not self.map.is_coastal(x, y):\n                return False\n        else:\n            # Other districts must be on land\n            if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                return False\n\n        # Must be within range of city center\n        if city_center:\n            cx, cy = city_center\n            # Calculate hex distance (simplified - use Manhattan as approximation)\n            distance = self.hex_distance(x, y, cx, cy)\n            if distance > max_range:\n                return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def hex_distance(self, x1: int, y1: int, x2: int, y2: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate distance between two hex tiles.\"\"\"\n        # Convert offset to cube coordinates\n        def offset_to_cube(x, y):\n            q = x\n            r = y - (x - (x & 1)) // 2\n            s = -q - r\n            return (q, r, s)\n\n        q1, r1, s1 = offset_to_cube(x1, y1)\n        q2, r2, s2 = offset_to_cube(x2, y2)\n\n        return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(r1 - r2) + abs(s1 - s2)) // 2\n\n    def optimize_single_city(self, population: int) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Optimize district placement for a single city.\"\"\"\n        max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n        # Find best city center position\n        best_city_center = None\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        # Try different city center positions on land tiles\n        for y in range(self.map.height):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                city_center = (x, y)\n                solution, score = self.optimize_districts_for_city(\n                    city_center, max_districts\n                )\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_city_center = city_center\n                    best_solution = solution\n\n        return {\n            'city_center': list(best_city_center),\n            'placements': {k: list(v) for k, v in best_solution['placements'].items()},\n            'adjacency_bonuses': best_solution['adjacency_bonuses'],\n            'total_adjacency': best_solution['total_adjacency']\n        }\n\n    def optimize_districts_for_city(self, city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                                   max_districts: int) -> Tuple[Dict, int]:\n        \"\"\"Optimize which districts to build and where for a given city center.\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n\n        # Get all valid positions within city range\n        valid_positions = []\n        cx, cy = city_center\n        for y in range(max(0, cy - 3), min(self.map.height, cy + 4)):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if (x, y) == city_center:\n                    continue\n                for district in self.DISTRICTS:\n                    if self.is_valid_placement(district, x, y, city_center, placements):\n                        valid_positions.append((x, y))\n                        break  # Only need to check if position is valid for any district\n\n        # Remove duplicates\n        valid_positions = list(set(valid_positions))\n\n        # Use greedy approach with lookahead for optimization\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        # Get map dimensions\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT Width, Height FROM Map\")\n        self.width, self.height = cursor.fetchone()\n\n        # Load terrain\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, TerrainType FROM Plots\")\n        self.terrain = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n        # Load features\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, FeatureType FROM PlotFeatures\")\n        self.features = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n        # Load rivers\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, IsNEOfRiver, IsWOfRiver, IsNWOfRiver FROM PlotRivers\")\n        for row in cursor.fetchall():\n            plot_id, ne, w, nw = row\n            if ne or w or nw:\n                self.rivers[plot_id] = {'ne': ne, 'w': w, 'nw': nw}\n\n        # Load resources\n        cursor.execute(\"SELECT ID, ResourceType FROM PlotResources\")\n        self.resources = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}\n\n        conn.close()\n\n    def plot_id_to_coords(self, plot_id: int) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n        \"\"\"Convert plot ID to (x, y) coordinates.\"\"\"\n        x = plot_id % self.width\n        y = plot_id // self.width\n        return (x, y)\n\n    def coords_to_plot_id(self, x: int, y: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Convert (x, y) coordinates to plot ID.\"\"\"\n        return y * self.width + x\n\n    def get_hex_neighbors(self, x: int, y: int) -> List[Tuple[int, int]]:\n        \"\"\"Get all valid neighboring tiles in hex grid (odd-q vertical layout).\"\"\"\n        neighbors = []\n\n        # Odd-q vertical layout - odd columns shifted down\n        if x % 2 == 0:  # Even column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 0),   # E\n                (1, -1),  # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, -1), # W\n                (-1, 0),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n        else:  # Odd column\n            offsets = [\n                (1, 1),   # E\n                (1, 0),   # NE\n                (0, -1),  # NW\n                (-1, 0),  # W\n                (-1, 1),  # SW\n                (0, 1)    # SE\n            ]\n\n        for dx, dy in offsets:\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            # Handle wrap-around for X if needed\n            if nx < 0:\n                nx += self.width\n            elif nx >= self.width:\n                nx -= self.width\n\n            # Check Y bounds (no wrap)\n            if 0 <= ny < self.height:\n                neighbors.append((nx, ny))\n\n        return neighbors\n\n    def is_mountain(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is a mountain.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return 'MOUNTAIN' in terrain\n\n    def is_coastal(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is coastal (not ocean, but adjacent to ocean/coast).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain == 'TERRAIN_COAST'\n\n    def is_land(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is buildable land (not water, not mountain).\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        terrain = self.terrain.get(plot_id, '')\n        return terrain not in ['TERRAIN_OCEAN', 'TERRAIN_COAST'] and 'MOUNTAIN' not in terrain\n\n    def has_river(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if tile is adjacent to a river.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return plot_id in self.rivers\n\n    def get_feature(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get feature at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.features.get(plot_id)\n\n    def get_resource(self, x: int, y: int) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Get resource at this tile.\"\"\"\n        plot_id = self.coords_to_plot_id(x, y)\n        return self.resources.get(plot_id)\n\n\nclass DistrictOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Optimizes district placement for maximum adjacency bonuses.\"\"\"\n\n    # District types that can be built\n    DISTRICTS = [\n        'CAMPUS',\n        'HOLY_SITE',\n        'THEATER_SQUARE',\n        'COMMERCIAL_HUB',\n        'HARBOR',\n        'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE',\n        'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX'\n    ]\n\n    def __init__(self, civ_map: Civ6Map):\n        self.map = civ_map\n\n    def calculate_adjacency(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str]) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate adjacency bonus for a district at position (x, y).\"\"\"\n        neighbors = self.map.get_hex_neighbors(x, y)\n        total = 0\n\n        if district == 'CAMPUS':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Rainforest/Jungle: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_JUNGLE':\n                    total += 1\n                # Reef: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_REEF':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'HOLY_SITE':\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Mountains: +2\n                if self.map.is_mountain(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Woods/Forest: +1\n                elif self.map.get_feature(nx, ny) == 'FEATURE_FOREST':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): -1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total -= 1\n\n        elif district == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Districts (not City Center): +0.5 each (rounds down every 2)\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total = district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # River: +2 per adjacent river tile\n                if self.map.has_river(nx, ny):\n                    total += 2\n                # Harbor district: +1\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'HARBOR':\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] != 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'HARBOR':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # City Center: +2\n                if (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] == 'CITY_CENTER':\n                    total += 2\n                # Coastal resources: +1\n                elif self.map.is_coastal(nx, ny) and self.map.get_resource(nx, ny):\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts: +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n            district_count = 0\n            for nx, ny in neighbors:\n                # Strategic resources: +1\n                resource = self.map.get_resource(nx, ny)\n                if resource and 'STRATEGIC' in resource:\n                    total += 1\n                # Other districts (not City Center, not Aqueduct): +0.5 each\n                elif (nx, ny) in placements and placements[(nx, ny)] not in ['CITY_CENTER', 'AQUEDUCT']:\n                    district_count += 1\n            total += district_count // 2\n\n        elif district == 'ENTERTAINMENT_COMPLEX':\n            # No standard adjacency bonuses from terrain\n            pass\n\n        return total\n\n    def is_valid_placement(self, district: str, x: int, y: int,\n                          city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                          placements: Dict[Tuple[int, int], str],\n                          max_range: int = 3) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a district can be placed at (x, y).\"\"\"\n        # Can't place on occupied tile\n        if (x, y) in placements:\n            return False\n\n        # Harbor must be on coastal tile\n        if district == 'HARBOR':\n            if not self.map.is_coastal(x, y):\n                return False\n        else:\n            # Other districts must be on land\n            if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                return False\n\n        # Must be within range of city center\n        if city_center:\n            cx, cy = city_center\n            # Calculate hex distance (simplified - use Manhattan as approximation)\n            distance = self.hex_distance(x, y, cx, cy)\n            if distance > max_range:\n                return False\n\n        return True\n\n    def hex_distance(self, x1: int, y1: int, x2: int, y2: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calculate distance between two hex tiles.\"\"\"\n        # Convert offset to cube coordinates\n        def offset_to_cube(x, y):\n            q = x\n            r = y - (x - (x & 1)) // 2\n            s = -q - r\n            return (q, r, s)\n\n        q1, r1, s1 = offset_to_cube(x1, y1)\n        q2, r2, s2 = offset_to_cube(x2, y2)\n\n        return (abs(q1 - q2) + abs(r1 - r2) + abs(s1 - s2)) // 2\n\n    def optimize_single_city(self, population: int) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Optimize district placement for a single city.\"\"\"\n        max_districts = (population - 1) // 3 + 1\n\n        # Find best city center position\n        best_city_center = None\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        # Try different city center positions on land tiles\n        for y in range(self.map.height):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if not self.map.is_land(x, y):\n                    continue\n\n                city_center = (x, y)\n                solution, score = self.optimize_districts_for_city(\n                    city_center, max_districts\n                )\n\n                if score > best_score:\n                    best_score = score\n                    best_city_center = city_center\n                    best_solution = solution\n\n        # Convert placements dict from {(x,y): district} to {district: [x,y]}\n        placements_output = {}\n        for (x, y), district in best_solution['placements'].items():\n            placements_output[district] = [x, y]\n\n        return {\n            'city_center': list(best_city_center),\n            'placements': placements_output,\n            'adjacency_bonuses': best_solution['adjacency_bonuses'],\n            'total_adjacency': best_solution['total_adjacency']\n        }\n\n    def optimize_districts_for_city(self, city_center: Tuple[int, int],\n                                   max_districts: int) -> Tuple[Dict, int]:\n        \"\"\"Optimize which districts to build and where for a given city center.\"\"\"\n        placements = {city_center: 'CITY_CENTER'}\n\n        # Get all valid positions within city range\n        valid_positions = []\n        cx, cy = city_center\n        for y in range(max(0, cy - 3), min(self.map.height, cy + 4)):\n            for x in range(self.map.width):\n                if (x, y) == city_center:\n                    continue\n                for district in self.DISTRICTS:\n                    if self.is_valid_placement(district, x, y, city_center, placements):\n                        valid_positions.append((x, y))\n                        break  # Only need to check if position is valid for any district\n\n        # Remove duplicates\n        valid_positions = list(set(valid_positions))\n\n        # Use greedy approach with lookahead for optimization\n        best_solution = None\n        best_score = -float('inf')\n\n        #
